The criterion points at FE-105 as a whole, so it stays open until FE-105 criterion 3 is met. `packages/element/e2e/list-semantics.spec.ts` ('three consecutive numbered paragraphs expose their position and the list size'; 'a paragraph at a nested numbering level exposes its level'), which reads `role="listitem"`, `aria-level`, `aria-posinset` and `aria-setsize` in the shadow root. Run with `pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/element/playwright.config.ts packages/element/e2e/list-semantics.spec.ts --project=<browser>` on port 56080: 2 of 2 passed in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit.

**Notes:** Tab inside a list competes with Tab for indent and next cell; the keyboard model is UX-013. Tab is tried first for a table cell, then the list level applies only with the caret at the start of a list item. Criterion 3 was open for want of a comparison with the PDF; lane w1 (2026-11-18) ticked it (see Evidence). Criterion 6 was ticked by lane U-FE-129-141 and unticked by the reviewer (see Evidence): FE-105 criterion 3 (a `role="list"` parent) was open. Lane w1 (2026-11-17) re-ticked it: decision 91 settled the list parent, and FE-105 criterion 3 is met. No screen reader was run: those passes are FE-126's. The restart and continue commands and the `applyList` `numberingId` argument are additive to `CommandName` and `CommandArgs`.

**Notes (lane V-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13):** criterion 6 is blocked by ProseMirror, not by a test. The list view (`packages/engine/src/view/list-view.ts`) gives each item `role="listitem"` as a node decoration, and a decoration cannot wrap sibling paragraphs in a `role="list"` element; a body node view cannot either, because ProseMirror maps a node view's children one to one. The fix is a rendering change to the body, which needs a decision (ADR 0308, item 6). Criterion 3 is blocked by the missing PDF output: no package writes a PDF, so the labels cannot be compared with one (ADR 0308, item 7). Both stay unticked.

### FE-131 Apply table styles and sort rows

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | M | should | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (tables with styles and sorting); 03 §4.3, §4.5 (table properties) · **Requirements:** R1, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-040, CDM-017, CDM-028

As an author, I want to give a table a style and sort its rows, so that tables look consistent and are easy to scan.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a table I may edit, when I apply a table style, then the table references it and its borders and shading follow the style unless overridden.
- [x] Given a column, when I sort ascending or descending, then the body rows are reordered by that column's text and repeated header rows stay at the top.
- [x] Given merged cells that cross the rows being sorted, then sorting is unavailable and the reason is given.
- [x] From phase 2, given I may only suggest, when I sort, then the reordering is proposed as whole-row suggestions (FE-045) or the command is unavailable, as PRM-062 settles.
- [x] Given a sort, when it is undone, then the original order returns in one step.

**Evidence:** criterion 2 and criterion 5: `packages/engine/src/table-style-sort.ts` (`sortTableRows`, `sortedOrder`, `sortRefusal`) and `packages/engine/test/table-style-sort.test.ts` ('sorts the body rows ascending by the caret column, with numbers in numeric order', 'sorts descending', 'keeps the header rows at the top', 'is one undo step: undo returns the original order', 'keeps the same row nodes and identifiers', and the property 'sortedOrder (property)'). Criterion 3 (stricter than the story): 'refuses a table with merged cells, with the reason, and changes nothing' and, through `queryCommand`, 'sorts through exec, and reports merged cells through queryCommand'. Criterion 1 is not ticked: `setTableStyle` stores the table's `style` reference (test 'stores the table style on the table at the caret and clears it with null'; 'refuses a style the document does not define as a table style'), but the borders and shading that follow it are the model's cascade and the web view's, which are not checked here.

**Notes:** phase inferred: 11 §4 places tables in phase 1b but names neither table styles nor sorting; sorting could move to phase 2. Engine lane 2 (round 3, 2026-11-13): the commands `setTableStyle` and `sortTableRows` are registered in `packages/engine/src/core.ts` (`exec`, `queryCommand`) with `CommandArgs.tableStyle`, `sortDirection` and `sortColumn`, and `CommandUnavailable` gained `unknown-style` and `no-column` (additive). Decisions not in the spec, recorded in `docs/adr/0131-engine-2-backlog-batch.md`: empty cells sort last in both directions; equal keys keep their order; text compares with a numeric-aware `en-GB` collator; a table with any merged cell refuses the sort, which is stricter than criterion 3. Criterion 4 is open: suggest mode (FE-045, PRM-062) is not in the core. Criterion 1 is open, see Evidence. Round 4, lane FE-131-141 (2026-11-13): the model's table cascade (`packages/model/src/cascade/table-format.ts`, tests in `table-format.test.ts`) gives a table its style's borders and cell margins, and a direct value overrides them; it has no shading, so the criterion stays unticked for shading and for the web view. Rows move as the same nodes inside one `replaceWith`; how the authority checks identifiers of moved rows is not tested here.

**Notes (lane V-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13):** the shading half of criterion 1 is made in the cascade. `resolveTableFormat` in `packages/model/src/cascade/table-format.ts` returns `headerRowShading` from the table style chain, with the most specific style winning (`packages/model/src/cascade/v-fe-129-141-table-shading.test.ts`, 3 tests passed in Node.js). Criterion 1 stays unticked: the web view does not draw the shading yet, and a table has no direct shading to override it. Decisions recorded in `docs/adr/0308-lane-V-FE-129-141.md`.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-15, criterion 1):** `packages/engine/src/view/table-style-view.ts` (`tableCellStyles`, `borderCss`, `tableStylePlugin`, `tableCellView`), wired into the body view in `packages/engine/src/view/web.ts`. `packages/engine/src/view/fe-131-table-style.universal.test.ts`: 'references the style when it is applied, and the borders and shading follow it' (through `exec('setTableStyle')`; the header row takes the base style's shading, the outer and inside borders come from the style, a cell's own shading and a cell's own border side win), 'lets the borders set on the table itself replace the borders of the style', 'gives a table without a style only what its cells set themselves', 'draws nothing from a value that does not match its grammar', and in the mounted body view 'draws the borders and shading of the applied style, and what a cell overrides' (computed styles of the cells). Run on 2026-11-15 with `pnpm exec vitest run --project browser packages/engine/src/view/fe-131-table-style.universal.test.ts`: Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 15 passed; in Node.js 4 passed and the view test is skipped. The readings the spec leaves open (border width unit, default width, `thick`, a border without a colour, `bandedRows`, the order of overrides) are proposed in `docs/adr/0365-w1-FE-131-table-style-drawing.md`.

**Notes (lane w1, 2026-11-15):** criterion 1 is ticked for the web view, the only view FE-131 names. `bandedRows` draws nothing (no band colour in the CDM), and tables in the header and footer panels do not take the style's drawing yet. Criterion 4 is ticked on its second branch: under suggestion mode the sort is unavailable with the reason `suggestion` and `run` changes nothing (`packages/engine/src/core.ts`; `packages/engine/test/fe-131-sort-suggest.test.ts`, 2 tests, a core in review mode). The first branch (whole-row suggestions) is not built. Which branch version 1 keeps is the owner's when PRM-062 is settled; the default taken is proposed in ADR 0365, point 7.

**Notes (lane FE-131-141, round 5, 2026-11-13):** criterion 1 stays unticked. The table takes its style through the `style` attribute (`setTableStyle`), and `resolveTableFormat` in `packages/model/src/cascade/table-format.ts` resolves the borders and the cell margins of that style, with a direct value winning. It does not resolve shading, so shading does not follow the style yet. The fix belongs in the model package, which this lane does not own, so it is recorded here and not made. Criterion 4 (phase 2, suggested row moves) is still open for PRM-062.

### FE-132 Insert pictures and figures with captions and alternative text

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | M | must | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (pictures); 03 §4.3 (`figure`, `caption`), §4.4 (`image`), §8 (I10); 10 §3.5 · **Requirements:** R1, R11, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-053, CDM-029, BE-042, BE-044, PRO-061

As an author, I want to insert a picture inline or as a captioned figure with alternative text, so that documents carry images that are accessible and typeset well.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a picture file from my device, when I insert it, then it is uploaded through quarantine as an asset (BE-042) and the document references the asset identifier, never embedded data (03 §4.4).
- [x] Given an inline image or a figure, when I resize it with a handle or the size dialog, then `width` and `height` are stored in twentieths of a point and the aspect ratio is kept unless I unlock it.
- [x] Given a figure, when I set its placement (`here`, `float`) and alignment, then they are stored; a floating figure inside a table cell is refused (I10).
- [x] Given a figure, when I add a caption, then the caption holds a `seq` field so it numbers itself (CDM-036).
- [x] Given an image without alternative text, when I insert it, then I am prompted for it, and the accessibility checker reports it later (UX-066).
- [x] Given the upload fails validation, then nothing is inserted and the reason is shown.

**Evidence:** criteria 1, 3, 4 and 6 (criteria 1, 4 and 6 added in lane FE-131-141, round 3, 2026-11-13). Criterion 1, the upload: `packages/engine/src/image-paste.ts` (`pasteImage`) hands the picture to the assets port, which quarantines and validates it, and inserts an image that names the asset; `packages/engine/test/image-paste.test.ts` ('uploads the picture through the port and inserts an image that names the asset', 'the document holds the asset identifier and the sizes, never the bytes') and `packages/engine/test/image-paste-core.test.ts` ('uploads through the assets port and inserts an image that names the asset'). The file chooser that calls it is UX's and is not built. Criterion 4, the caption's `seq` field: `packages/engine/test/captions-refs.test.ts` ('adds a caption to a figure that has none, numbered in the Figure sequence') and `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` ('numbers a new figure caption and refers to it', through `DocumentCore`). Criterion 6, a failed upload: `packages/engine/test/image-paste.test.ts` ('returns the message of a rejected picture and leaves the document as it was'). Criterion 3 as before: `packages/engine/src/figure-layout.ts` (`setFigureLayout`, `figureRefusal`) and `packages/engine/test/figure-layout.test.ts` ('stores a floating placement and an alignment in the body'; 'refuses a floating figure inside a table cell (I10) and stores nothing'; 'refuses a placement the CDM does not allow'; 'reports a figure in a table cell as floating-refused through queryCommand'). The command is registered in `packages/engine/src/core.ts` (`setFigureLayout`, `exec` and `queryCommand`). Criterion 2 is not ticked: `resizeFigure` and `sizeFor` store the twip size with the aspect ratio kept or unlocked (test 'keeps the aspect ratio when the width is set, and stores twips', and a property over locked widths), but the story's handles and size dialog are the view's.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-17, criterion 2):** the handle is built, the size dialog is not (the criterion says "a handle or the size dialog"). `packages/engine/src/view/resize-handle.ts` (a focusable `role="slider"` in the picture's corner; a drag or the arrow keys call `resizeFigure`, so the stored `width` and `height` are twips, 15 per CSS pixel, and the command's range and scope rules apply; the aspect ratio is kept, and Shift unlocks it), `packages/engine/src/view/figure-view.ts` and `packages/engine/src/view/image-view.ts` (node views that draw the stored size and carry the handle), registered in `packages/engine/src/view/web.ts`; `resizeFigure` in `packages/engine/src/figure-layout.ts` now also accepts an inline `image`; styles in `packages/element/src/styles/element.css`. Tests: `packages/engine/src/view/fe-132-resize.universal.test.ts` (8 tests, synthetic pointer and key events: stored size drawn, drag keeps the ratio, Shift unlocks it, a refused or cancelled drag restores, keys step, inline image, a view in view mode changes nothing; `pnpm exec vitest run --project browser <file>`: 24 passed in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit on 2026-11-17), `packages/engine/test/figure-layout.test.ts` ('resizes an inline image too, but gives it no placement'), and `packages/element/e2e/fe-132-resize.spec.ts` (a real mouse and keyboard on the S1 page: a 40 pixel drag stores 3600 by 1800 twips from 3000 by 1500, a Shift release stores 3900 by 1650, ArrowLeft stores 3750 by 1587; 3 of 3 passed in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit on 2026-11-17, port 57101).

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-18, criterion 5):** a picture that is uploaded and inserted now raises a request for its alternative text, and the shell asks for it in a dialog. Core: `packages/engine/src/alt-text.ts` (`setAltText`, `imageWithoutAlt`), `DocumentCore.altRequest` (set after each inserted picture) and `DocumentCore.setImageAlt` (through the scope guard) in `packages/engine/src/core.ts`; `packages/engine/test/fe-132-alt-text.test.ts` (5 passed, Node.js: one request per inserted picture naming its asset, none when the upload fails, the text is stored trimmed on the first picture of that asset without text, two pictures take two answers, blank text, an unknown asset and a view-only body are refused). Shell: `packages/ui/src/components/AltTextDialog.svelte` (a text area, Apply, and Later), opened by `packages/ui/src/App.svelte` on the request and applied through the named command `setAltText` (`packages/element/src/editor.ts`, `packages/ui/src/core-api.ts`); strings in `packages/ui/src/i18n/en-GB.ts`. The element now hands the shell `getContent`, so the accessibility checker pane is available in it (before, `panes.ts` hid the pane for want of `getContent`). In a real browser: `packages/element/e2e/fe-132-alt-prompt.spec.ts` (a picture file pasted on the surface with an assets port stand-in: the dialog opens, Apply is off until there is text, Apply stores the text on the picture; Later leaves the picture without text and the checker pane, opened through `openPane('checker')`, lists it as an `alt-text` issue). Run on 2026-11-18 on Windows 11 with `$env:DOCEDIT_DEMO_PORT="57622"; pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/element/playwright.config.ts packages/element/e2e/fe-132-alt-prompt.spec.ts --retries=0`: 6 passed in Chromium, Edge and WebKit; Firefox is skipped because it ignores the clipboardData of a synthetic paste (as in `sec-005-image-paste.spec.ts`). The checker half is UX-066 criterion 1 and 3 (`packages/ui/test/ux-066-export-agreement.test.ts`).

**Notes:** 13 §2 lists cropping as built in, but the CDM has no crop attributes (03 §4.3, §4.4). Recorded in the findings note of 2026-11-06; not decided here. Engine lane 2 (round 3, 2026-11-13): criterion 2 stays open for the handles and the size dialog (UX), though the engine stores the size. Criterion 4 (a caption with a `seq` field) is met at module level by `insertCaption` in `packages/engine/src/captions-refs.ts` (FE-134's module, `captions-refs.test.ts`); it is not ticked here, because that command is not in the core's command table yet and FE-134 owns it. Round 3 (lane FE-131-141): criteria 1, 4 and 6 are ticked, see Evidence. Criterion 5 needs the accessibility prompt (UX) and is not built. Lane w1 (2026-11-17): criterion 2 is ticked on the handle alone. Criterion 5 was open for want of the prompt; lane w1 (2026-11-18) ticked it (see Evidence): the prompt follows the insertion paths that exist, which are paste and drop of a picture file (`pasteImage`); a file chooser (a toolbar command that picks a file from the device) is still not built, and when it is it should call the same `pasteFromClipboard` path, so that the prompt follows. The checker half exists (UX-066). Readings taken for the handle, not stated by the spec: the unlock is Shift (on release for a drag, with the arrow keys for a step); a step is 10 pixels; the smallest size a drag or key sets is 1 pixel (15 twips). An inline image's handle sits inside its `role="img"` element, whose children are presentational, so a screen reader may not announce it; the arrow keys work when it has focus. Decisions not in the spec, recorded in `docs/adr/0131-engine-2-backlog-batch.md`: a size is refused outside 1 to 31680 twips rather than clamped, and with the aspect ratio locked and both values given the width is used.

### FE-133 Insert links and bookmarks

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | S | must | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2; 03 §4.4 (`anchor`), §5 (`link`), §8 (I4, I9) · **Requirements:** R1, R14 · **Depends on:** CDM-036, CDM-038, FE-067

As an author, I want links to web pages and to places in the document, and bookmarks to link to, so that readers can navigate.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a selection, when I add a link, then the URL is checked against the safe schemes of I9 before it is stored, and an unsafe one is refused with a reason.
- [x] Given a bookmark name, when I insert a bookmark, then an anchor with that name is created, and a duplicate name is refused.
- [x] Given a link to a bookmark or a heading, when I follow it in the editor, then the caret moves there.
- [x] Given a link to a web page, when I open it from the editor, then it opens in a new browsing context without an opener (FE-067).
- [x] Given a bookmark that a cross-reference targets, when I delete it, then the editor explains that it is still referenced (I4) and offers to show the references.

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13, criterion 4):** `packages/engine/src/fe-133-link-open.universal.test.ts` ('opens an https link in a new browsing context without an opener'; 'keeps an in-document bookmark link in the current context'), run with `pnpm vitest run --project browser packages/engine/src/fe-133-link-open.universal.test.ts` (the browser project). The rendering is `MARK_RENDERING.link` in `packages/engine/src/schema.ts`, which now sets `target="_blank"` on an external link and leaves an in-document `#` link without a target. `packages/engine/src/sec-006-link-render.universal.test.ts` and `packages/engine/test/web-view-safety.test.ts` still pass.

**Evidence:** criteria 1 and 2: `packages/engine/src/core.ts` registers `addLink`, `insertBookmark` and `followInternalLink` as document API commands (`run`, `exec`, `queryCommand` with `href`, `title`, `name`; `AuthoringCommandName` in `api.ts`), with the region guard's `canWrite` for the scope of the position as the writable test; `queryCommand` gives the reason of a refusal (`scheme-not-allowed`, `malformed-link`, `duplicate-bookmark`, `bad-bookmark-name`, `no-access`). `packages/engine/test/authoring-commands.test.ts` goes through `DocumentCore`: a safe link is stored on the selection, `javascript:`, `data:` and `file:` targets are refused with `scheme-not-allowed` and nothing is changed or queued, a bookmark is created with its name and an identifier, a duplicate name is refused, and both commands are unavailable (`no-access`) in a region the real guard protects. The module's own rules: `packages/engine/src/links-bookmarks.ts` and `packages/engine/test/links-bookmarks.test.ts` (fast-check properties over schemes and bookmark names). Reviewed and unticked on 2026-11-13 because nothing reached the core; re-ticked after the commands were registered.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-17, criteria 3 and 5):** Criterion 3, headings: `packages/engine/src/links-bookmarks.ts` (`followInternalLink` falls back from a bookmark name to a heading's identifier; `headingPosition`, by the outline-level cascade) and `packages/engine/test/fe-133-heading-and-references.test.ts` ('follows #<heading id> to the start of the heading and changes nothing else', 'does not take a paragraph that is no heading, or an unknown identifier, as a target'; the bookmark half stays in `authoring-commands.test.ts`). Criterion 3, following in the editor: `packages/engine/src/view/web.ts` (`handleDOMEvents.click`: a Ctrl or Command click on an in-document link follows it while the text is editable, a plain click does where it is not, and the browser's own fragment jump is stopped) and `packages/engine/src/view/fe-133-follow-link.universal.test.ts` (a plain click leaves the caret, a Ctrl-click moves it to the start of the heading). Run on 2026-11-17 with `pnpm exec vitest run --project browser packages/engine/src/view/fe-133-follow-link.universal.test.ts` in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit: 3 passed. Criterion 5: `packages/engine/src/referenced-targets.ts` (the guard reports the refusal), `packages/engine/src/core.ts` (`#referenceRefused` records a `referenced` refusal with the number of references; `showReferences` selects them in turn, skipping scopes the reader may not view), `packages/engine/src/api.ts` (`RefusalReason`, `showReferences`), `packages/element/src/editor.ts` (named command `showReferences`), `packages/ui/src/shell/notices.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/StatusBar.svelte` ("Show references" button) and `packages/ui/src/i18n/en-GB.ts` (`notice.refused.referenced`, `notice.action.showReferences`). Tests: the engine file above ('records a `referenced` refusal with the number of references, and changes nothing', 'selects the references one after another, and the first again after the last', 'shows nothing when no deletion was refused for this reason, or when the references are gone', 'is silent for a reference that was removed together with its target'; `pnpm exec vitest run --project node packages/engine/test/fe-133-heading-and-references.test.ts`), `packages/ui/test/fe-133-referenced-notice.test.ts` (message, count, offer, singular and plural) and the browser case in `packages/ui/e2e/notices.spec.ts` ('a deletion a cross-reference names is explained and offers to show the references (FE-133)': the bar says it with the count, the live region announces it, axe finds nothing, the button runs `showReferences`, and another refusal has no button), run with `pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/ui/playwright.config.ts notices.spec --project=chromium`: 3 passed, 2026-11-17. The additive test-support changes are `FakeCore.refuse(..., count)`, the harness's `refuse`, and `showReferences` in `FAKE_COMMANDS`. Proposed decisions: ADR 0377.

**Notes (lane w1, 2026-11-17):** Criterion 3 reads an in-document target as a bookmark name, else a heading paragraph's identifier (ADR 0377); the CDM's pattern already allows it and no spec text fixes another form, so this is proposed, not decided. Criterion 5 is not run end to end in one browser page: the engine half (refusal and `showReferences`) runs in Node.js and the shell half (notice and button, with a fake core) runs in Chromium. A page-level run needs a document with a labelled bookmark (a numbered list item or a caption), which the embed page's document does not have, and `insertReference` refuses a target without a label; `packages/element/src/editor.ts` maps `showReferences` to the core's method without a browser test of its own.

**Lane U-FE-129-141 (2026-11-13), criterion 4:** the core now refuses the deletion of a bookmark that a `ref` names (`referenced-targets.ts`, see FE-134 criterion 4). The explanation that it is still referenced and the offer to show the references need the view, so criterion 4 stays unticked.

**Engine-lane notes (round 1, not decided here):** criterion 3 is open for headings: the CDM defines no heading target for `#` links, so only bookmark targets are followed. Round 4, lane FE-131-141 (2026-11-13): the bookmark half is tested through the core in `packages/engine/test/authoring-commands.test.ts` ('following #name moves the caret to the bookmark and changes nothing else'); criterion 3 stays unticked for headings. Criterion 4 (opens without an opener) was closed by lane U-FE-129-141, see Evidence; before that the link mark had no `target`, so it did not open a new browsing context. Criterion 5 needs cross-reference fields (FE-134), which are not built. Bookmark names are unique case-sensitively, because the CDM states no case rule; this is a proposed reading for the owner to confirm, not a decision. The name pattern is copied from `cdm1.ts` because the definition index does not export it. The commands reach the document API (`exec` on the element); the toolbar and command registry entries are UX-003's and are not built.

### FE-134 Insert numbered captions and cross-references

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | M | should | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (numbered captions with cross-references); 03 §4.3, §4.4 (`field` kinds `seq`, `ref`), §7 · **Requirements:** R1, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-132, CDM-036, CDM-042, SPK-006

As an author, I want to number figures, tables and equations and refer to them from the text, so that references stay right when content moves.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a figure, a table or an equation, when I insert a caption, then a `seq` field numbers it within its sequence name (Figure, Table, Equation).
- [x] Given the cross-reference dialog, when I choose a bookmark, a heading, a numbered paragraph, a caption or an equation label, then a `ref` field targeting it is inserted in the chosen format.
- [x] Given content is moved or inserted before a captioned item, then caption numbers and the text of every reference update in the editor and match the PDF (CDM-042).
- [x] Given a reference whose target I try to delete, then the deletion is handled as I4 requires.
- [x] Given a field, then `cached` holds its last computed text for consumers that cannot compute it (03 §4.4).

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13, criterion 4; unticked by the round 5 review because `eq:` equation-label targets were not checked, re-ticked by the round 5 fixer once they were; `referenced-targets.ts` walks the whole document on every local edit, unmeasured):** `packages/engine/src/referenced-targets.ts` (`referenceGuardPlugin`, `removedReferencedTargets`; it resolves all three `ref` target forms, `node:`, `bookmark:` and `eq:<label>` to the `equation_block` with that `label`, as the model's validator does), installed in `packages/engine/src/core.ts` after the region guard. `packages/engine/test/fe-134-referenced-target.test.ts` (11 tests, Node.js: a figure that a `ref` names is not deleted, a figure no reference names is deleted, the reference alone may go, the reference and its figure go together, a bookmark that a `ref` names is not deleted, a remote transaction passes; a numbered equation that a `ref` names as `eq:energy` is not deleted, a numbered equation no `ref` names is deleted, an equation is deleted when the `ref` names another label, relabelling a referenced equation is refused, the `ref` and its equation go together) and `packages/engine/test/fe-134-core-target-delete.test.ts` (2 tests through `DocumentCore`: the bookmark, and the numbered equation `eq:energy`, each stay while their `ref` is in the document, and go once the reference is gone). Both run with `pnpm vitest run <file>` and passed. The refusal is silent: the explanation and the offer to show the references (FE-133 criterion 4) are the view's and are not built.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-17, criterion 3, on decision 90 of `docs/owner-decisions-2026-11-15.md`: refreshed caption and reference texts are not written into the document; they are computed when shown and when exported):** the editor: `packages/engine/src/captions-refs.ts` (`fieldTexts`: the computed text of every `seq` and `ref` field, from the model's `computeLabels` and `referenceText`; `staleCaches` is now a filter of it), `packages/engine/src/view/computed-field-view.ts` (a node view that shows that text and is drawn again after each change of the document) wired in `fieldView` of `packages/engine/src/view/form-field-view.ts`. `packages/engine/src/view/fe-134-computed-field.universal.test.ts` (figure B with a reference to it, every `cached` text 1: inserting figure A before B makes B's number and the reference show 2 while `cached` stays 1, and removing A takes them back to 1; `pnpm exec vitest run --project browser <file>`: 3 passed, Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 2026-11-17). The PDF: `packages/export-latex/src/fe-134-numbers.compile.test.ts` compiles the same document in the Podman sandbox and reads the PDF's text with `pdftotext`: one `Figure 1`, one `Figure 2` and `See 2 above.` although every `cached` text is 1 (`DOCEDIT_REQUIRE_SANDBOX=1 pnpm exec vitest run --project node <file>`: 1 passed in 101 s on 2026-11-17, on this Windows machine with a WSL Podman machine; without the variable the test skips when the sandbox probe times out on a loaded machine). Both sides read `computeLabels` (CDM-042).

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-18, criteria 1 and 2):** criterion 1: `packages/engine/src/captions-refs.ts` (`insertTableCaption`: a paragraph just before the table at the caret, in the style `Caption` where the document defines it, holding `Table ` and a `seq` field named `Table`; `insertEquationCaption`: sets `numbered` and, if the block has none, a `label` (`eq1`, `eq2` ...), so the model counts the block in the `Equation` sequence; `insertCaptionHere`: the figure, the table or the selected equation the selection is on), reached through `insertCaption` with the new argument `caption: { kind: 'table' | 'equation' | 'here', id? }` (`packages/engine/src/api.ts`, `core.ts`) and, from the shell, through the command `insert.caption` (`packages/ui/src/commands/builtin.ts`, `packages/element/src/editor.ts`). `packages/engine/test/fe-134-table-equation-captions.test.ts` (10 tests, Node.js, `pnpm exec vitest run --project node <file>`: 10 passed, 2026-11-18): the table caption is a `Table` seq paragraph numbered 1 above the table; tables count apart from figures; a second caption on the same table, a caption outside a table and a missing equation are refused with the document unchanged; two equations are numbered 1 and 2 in document order, and an equation given a number first takes the number its position gives; `doc.check()` passes. Criterion 2: `packages/engine/src/reference-targets.ts` (`referenceTargets`, `DocumentCore.referenceTargets()`: numbered headings, other numbered paragraphs, captions of every sequence, labelled numbered equations, and bookmarks on a numbered block, each with the text a reference prints), `packages/ui/src/components/CrossReferenceDialog.svelte` (a kind filter, the list of targets as radio buttons, the format `The number` or `The page number`, Insert off until a target is chosen), the command `insert.crossReference` (opens the dialog; `packages/ui/src/commands/builtin.ts`, `App.svelte`), the named command `insertCrossReference` (`{ target, format }`, run as `insertReference`) and `referenceTargets` in `packages/element/src/editor.ts`. The format `page` is the one the PDF export reads (`packages/export-latex/src/inline.ts`, `DEpref`); the web view shows the number. `packages/engine/test/fe-134-table-equation-captions.test.ts` also checks the list (a table caption, a figure caption, a labelled equation, a bookmark; nothing for an unnumbered paragraph or equation) and that `insertReference` accepts every listed target with the label as `cached`. `packages/element/e2e/fe-134-cross-reference.spec.ts` (the real element with `toolbar="full"`: the table at the caret and the equation block are numbered, the Insert tab's Cross-reference button opens the dialog, the list shows `Table 1, prints 1` and `eq1, prints 1`, the filter narrows it, Insert is off until a target is chosen, choosing the equation with the page number format stores a `ref` to `eq:eq1` with format `page`, and a second to the table caption stores a `node:` target; `DOCEDIT_DEMO_PORT=58134 pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/element/playwright.config.ts packages/element/e2e/fe-134-cross-reference.spec.ts --project=chromium --project=firefox --project=webkit --retries=0 --workers=1`: 3 passed, 2026-11-18). The parity row `lists` gained its test links in `packages/ui/src/parity/tests.ts` (FE-130's row was shipped without them and failed `parity-report.test.ts`); `spec/parity` and `spec/embedding` were regenerated for the two new commands.

**Evidence:** criterion 5 (lane FE-131-141, round 3, 2026-11-13): `packages/engine/test/captions-refs.test.ts` ('inserts a ref to a captioned figure, with the number as its cached text'; 'renumbers a captioned figure when a figure is inserted before it, and refreshes its cache') and `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` ('numbers a new figure caption and refers to it', through `DocumentCore`, which reads back the `cached` text of the `seq` and `ref` fields).

**Notes:** the first market (SPK-006) sets how much cross-references matter in phase 1b. Engine lane, round 2 (2026-11-13, not decided here): `packages/engine/src/captions-refs.ts` inserts a figure caption (`insertCaption`: a `seq` field named `Figure` whose `cached` is the number that `computeLabels` gives), inserts a `ref` field to a `node:`, `bookmark:` or `eq:` target (`insertReference`, refused for a target with no label) and finds stale `cached` texts (`staleCaches`, `refreshCaches`). Tests: `packages/engine/test/captions-refs.test.ts`, passed. Nothing is ticked. Criterion 1 is open for tables (the CDM has no table caption node) and for equations (the CDM numbers them through `numbered`, not a `seq` field). Criterion 2 needs the cross-reference dialog (UX). Criterion 3 needs the editor to refresh `cached` on each change (criterion 5 is ticked, see Evidence); whether that step goes into the committed document is a sync decision not made here. Criterion 4 needed I4 for targets, since built (`referenced-targets.ts`, see Evidence). Lane w1 (2026-11-18): criteria 1 and 2 are ticked (see Evidence); the statements below that they stay open are superseded. Readings taken, not stated by the spec: a table caption is a paragraph with a `seq` field named `Table` before the table (the CDM has no table caption node, the same reading as the list of tables of FE-136), so it is a paragraph, not a node the table owns, and deleting the table leaves it; a table has no identifier, so "the table" is the outermost table the caret is in; an equation is numbered through `numbered` and labelled `eq<n>` automatically (the model counts equations in the `Equation` sequence, CDM-030, and a `ref` to an equation can only use `eq:<label>`); the dialog offers only targets that have a label, so an unnumbered heading is not offered, because a `ref` prints a number and an unnumbered heading has none (a heading is offered once its style or paragraph is numbered); the two formats are "the number" (format null) and "the page number" (format `page`, which the PDF prints and the web view shows as the number, since it has no pages); `referenceTargets` walks the document on each call while the dialog opens, unmeasured; a caption command for the Equation kind needs the equation to be selected as a node or named by identifier. The toolbar has no icon for either command. Earlier: lane w1 (2026-11-17): criterion 3 is ticked on decision 90 (see Evidence). Criterion 1 stayed open: the CDM has no table caption node and numbers equations through `numbered`, not a `seq` field, and no decision of 2026-11-15 changes that. Criterion 2 stays open: the cross-reference dialog is UX's and is not built. Readings taken, not stated by the spec: a `ref` whose target has no label keeps its `cached` text in the editor with a `data-unresolved` mark, where the PDF prints nothing and reports `ref_unresolved`; `fieldTexts` walks the document once for each version while a `seq` or `ref` field is on screen, and its cost on a large document is not measured. The commands are not yet in the core's command table (`core.ts`, `api.ts`); see the batch record in `docs/adr/0131-engine-2-backlog-batch.md`.

### FE-135 Insert breaks, page sections, columns and footnotes

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | M | must | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (columns, breaks, footnotes); 03 §4.2 (`section_break`), §4.4 (`break`, `footnote`), §8 (I10), §10; 07 §5.2 (P2); 02 §7 · **Requirements:** R1, R3, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-058, CDM-020, CDM-033, CDM-034, PRM-011

As an author, I want to insert page, column and line breaks, start a new page section, set columns and add footnotes, so that I control the structure of the printed document.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given the caret in body text, when I insert a page, column or line break, then a `break` node of that kind is inserted; page and column breaks inside cells, captions and footnotes are refused (I10).
- [x] Given the caret in the body outside every region, when I insert a section break with a start type (`nextPage`, `continuous`, `evenPage`, `oddPage`), then a `section_break` with its own page setup becomes a direct child of `body`; this needs `structure` (P2, PRM-011).
- [x] Given a page section, when I set the number of columns and the gap, then its page setup stores them.
- [x] Given the caret, when I insert a footnote, then it gets the next number and opens in the footnote panel (FE-058); a footnote inside a footnote or a caption is refused (I10).
- [x] Given these structures in the web view, then they are marked without pretending to show pages (02 §7).
- [x] Given a continuous section break that changes margins, then the editor warns that the PDF will report it as unsupported (03 §10).

**Evidence:** criterion 1 is unticked by review (2026-11-13): `insertBreak` is tested only through its own module and is not in the core's command table or the package's exports, so no user path inserts a break yet; the I10 refusals are tested and become evidence once the command is wired. Criterion 3 stays ticked (`setPageSetup` is wired and accepts `columns` and `columnGap`). Criteria 1 and 3: `packages/engine/src/breaks-sections.ts` (`insertBreak`, `insertSectionBreak`, `sectionWarnings`) and `packages/engine/src/page-setup-commands.ts` (`setPageSetup`, unchanged). `packages/engine/test/breaks-sections.test.ts` (13 tests, passed 2026-11-13): a page, column and line break is inserted in body text; a page or column break in a table cell, a caption or a footnote is refused (`in-cell`, `in-caption`, `in-footnote`) and nothing is dispatched; a line break is allowed in each of those places; a fast-check property covers every break kind and place. Criterion 3: `setPageSetup` stores `columns` 2 and `columnGap` 720 on the page setup of the section. Round 1, lane FE-131-141 (2026-11-13): the statement above that `insertBreak` is not in the core's command table is out of date; `core.ts` registers it (`AUTHORING`, `#authoring`). Criterion 1 is ticked on `packages/engine/test/fe-135-136-core-authoring.test.ts` (a page, column and line break becomes a `break` node in body text through `DocumentCore.run`; a page or column break in a table cell or a footnote is refused and nothing changes, a line break is allowed; a region the real guard protects, no argument and view mode refuse it). Fixer round 2 (B group 1, 2026-11-13): `insertBreak` and `insertSectionBreak` are `AuthoringCommandName`s in `packages/engine/src/api.ts` (argument `breakKind`, `section`), exported from `packages/engine/src/headless.ts`; `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` also runs criterion 1 through `exec` and `queryCommand` (15 tests, passed 2026-11-13 with `pnpm exec vitest run packages/engine`, 474 passed). Criterion 2 (lane FE-131-141, round 3, 2026-11-13): `packages/engine/test/fe-135-section-starts.test.ts` ('inserts a section_break with that start type as a direct child of body, with its own page setup', run for `nextPage`, `continuous`, `evenPage` and `oddPage`; 'refuses a start type outside the four, and dispatches nothing'), with `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` ('adds a section break as a child of the body and its own page setup, for the owner') and its refusal of `structure` (P2).

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13, criterion 4):** `packages/element/e2e/fe-135-footnote.spec.ts` ('the new footnote takes the next number and opens in the panel'), run with `pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/element/playwright.config.ts e2e/fe-135-footnote.spec.ts` (Chromium, Firefox, WebKit and Edge: 4 passed). The S1 page's Insert footnote button inserts a footnote after the first; the body shows `data-n` 1 and 2 with labels `Footnote 1` and `Footnote 2`, the panel's sub-editor takes the typed text, and the validator reports no violation. The refusal of a footnote in a footnote or a caption is in the engine's module tests (`packages/engine/test/footnote-panel.test.ts`) and the browser paste test for nesting (`packages/element/e2e/s1-footnote-model.spec.ts`, not re-run in this lane).

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, round 6, criterion 5):** `packages/engine/src/schema.ts` (`breakMarkDOM`: a page or column break is a non-editable inline label, `Page break` or `Column break`, with `role="separator"`; a line break stays `<br>`; the section break stays its `hr`) and `packages/engine/src/fe-135-break-mark.universal.test.ts` (4 tests: the page break label and its box, the column break label and the line break, the section break mark with its start type, the inline size of the label). Run with `pnpm vitest run --project browser packages/engine/src/fe-135-break-mark.universal.test.ts`: Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 12 passed. Round 6 fix: the break node also parses `span.de-break[data-kind]` (page and column only) back to the same break, so a break serialised by ProseMirror's own clipboard path no longer pastes as its label text; `packages/engine/src/fe-135-break-clipboard.universal.test.ts` (7 tests: each of page, column and line through the schema parser and through `__parseFromClipboard` on a live view, and an unknown kind left as text), Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 21 passed. The existing node tests for breaks and sections still pass (`breaks-sections`, `layout-commands`, `fe-135-136-core-authoring`, `paste-section-break`, `fe-135-section-starts`, `fe-135-section-warning-core`: 38 passed).

**Evidence (lane V-FE-129-141, round 4, criterion 2):** `packages/engine/test/fe-135-section-after-sources.test.ts` (1 test, Node.js, `pnpm vitest run packages/engine/test/fe-135-section-after-sources.test.ts`, passed): with a `source_def` in `defs`, the new `page_setup` goes after the last page setup, the document still passes `doc.check()`, and the section names the new setup. The fault the audit found is fixed in `packages/engine/src/breaks-sections.ts` (`pageSetupInsertPos`). The client guard for `structure` is tested in `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` (run with the other section tests: 31 passed with `breaks-sections.test.ts`, `fe-135-section-starts.test.ts`). The authority's check of P2 on commit has no test here.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-17, criterion 6):** `packages/engine/src/view/section-warning-view.ts` (`sectionWarningPlugin`, registered in `packages/engine/src/view/web.ts`) draws a non-editable `role="note"` after the mark of each break that `sectionWarnings` reports, saying "This continuous section break changes the margins. The PDF will report it as unsupported."; style in `packages/element/src/styles/element.css`. `packages/engine/src/view/fe-135-section-warning.universal.test.ts` (2 tests on a live body view: no note after a continuous break alone; the note appears right after the break's mark when the section's `marginTop` changes and goes when it is restored; a next-page break with the same change has no note; the note is not in the document text; `pnpm exec vitest run --project browser <file>`: 6 passed, Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 2026-11-17). The warning rule is the core's (`packages/engine/test/fe-135-section-warning-core.test.ts`).

**Notes:** Lane w1 (2026-11-17): criterion 6 is ticked; the view now shows the warning (see Evidence, which supersedes the statements below that nothing shows it). The note is in the page flow beside the mark; it does not say which margin changed. Whether the PDF export does report such a break as unsupported is the export's (EXP) and was not re-checked here. Earlier notes follow. Criterion 2 was unticked by audit (2026-11-13): `insertSectionBreak` (`packages/engine/src/breaks-sections.ts`) inserts the new `page_setup` at the end of `defs`, which the content expression (`page_setup+ role_def* rule_def* source_def*`) does not allow after a `source_def`, `role_def` or `rule_def`, so in such a document (for example the authoring test document, which has `source_def` entries) `exec('insertSectionBreak')` returns true, inserts the `section_break`, silently drops the `page_setup`, and leaves `pageSetup` naming a definition that does not exist (I4); the existing tests use a document whose `defs` end with a `page_setup`, so they do not see it. Earlier note, superseded: criterion 2 was ticked in round 3, see Evidence. The module checks the position (body text, outside every region) and builds the new `page_setup` as a copy of the caret section's, without its header and footer stories. The P2 right (`structure`, 07 §5.2) is enforced by the authority; the client guard also refuses it, and `layout-commands.test.ts` shows `exec('insertSectionBreak')` refused with `structure` for an access without it and done for an owner. The criterion was not re-ticked in the fixer round, which may only re-tick what the reviewer unticked; the next review can tick it on that test. Criterion 4 stays open: the refusal of a footnote inside a footnote or a caption is tested in `footnote-panel.test.ts`, but the next number and the opening of the panel are not tested here. Round 4, lane FE-131-141 (2026-11-13): the next number is tested on `packages/engine/test/fe-135-footnote-number.test.ts` (the numbers `footnoteNumbers()` shows follow document order after `insertFootnote` at the caret, and the inserted footnote takes its ordinal); the core names the panel target (`packages/engine/test/footnote-open.test.ts`). Mounting the panel is the view's (FE-058), so criterion 4 stays unticked. Criterion 5 is the web view's and is not built. Criterion 6 stays open: `sectionWarnings` computes the warning and nothing shows it yet. Decisions taken here, not in the spec, recorded in `docs/adr/0131-engine-2-backlog-batch.md`. The commands are in the core's command table (`core.ts`, `api.ts`); the core names the new `section_break` and `page_setup` with its identifier source. Lane V-FE-129-141 (round 2, 2026-11-13), criterion 6 still open: `DocumentCore.sectionWarnings()` now returns the continuous-margin warning, tested in `packages/engine/test/fe-135-section-warning-core.test.ts` (vitest, Node.js, passed); the warning is not yet shown by the view, so the criterion is not ticked.

### FE-136 Insert fields, a table of contents, symbols and equations

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | M | must | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (page numbers, symbols, equations, table of contents, fields); 03 §4.3 (`toc`), §4.4 (`field`), §7; 09 §3.2 · **Requirements:** R1, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-057, FE-070, CDM-031, CDM-035

As an author, I want to insert page numbers, dates, the title, a table of contents, symbols and equations, so that common document furniture is one command away.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a header or footer, when I insert a page number or the page count, then a `field` of kind `page` or `pageCount` is inserted in the chosen format.
- [x] Given the body, when I insert a date or title field, then a `field` of that kind is inserted; its text comes from the shared computation and `cached` holds the last value (03 §4.4).
- [x] Given the body, when I insert a table of contents with level limits, or a list of figures or tables, then a `toc` atom is inserted and the web view shows its entries, updated as headings and captions change.
- [x] Given the symbol command, when I pick a character, then it is inserted as text, and a character the current font lacks is flagged before export (09 §3.2).
- [x] Given the equation command, when I insert an inline or block equation, then the math editor opens (FE-070).

**Evidence:** criterion 1 is unticked by review (2026-11-13): `insertField` is tested only through its own module and is not in the core's command table or the package's exports, so no user path inserts a page field yet; the tests become evidence once the command is wired. Criterion 1 as tested: `packages/engine/src/fields-toc.ts` (`insertField`, `insertToc`, `insertSymbol`) and `packages/engine/test/fields-toc.test.ts` (18 tests, passed 2026-11-13). A `page` or `pageCount` field is inserted in a header story with the chosen format stored and `cached` empty; in body text it is refused (`not-in-story`). A fast-check property covers every insertable kind and place. Criterion 2, the date half: a `date` field's `cached` is `dateFieldText`, the model's shared computation (CDM-035), in the workflow's zone (the test checks 23:30 UTC on 6 November is 07/11/2026 in Asia/Kolkata). The module reads no clock. Criterion 3 and criterion 4 are tested for insertion only: a `toc` atom with its level limits and `of` goes after the caret block, in the body or a region; a symbol is inserted as text, one code point at a time. Round 1, lane FE-131-141 (2026-11-13): the statement above that `insertField` is not in the core's command table is out of date; `core.ts` registers it (`AUTHORING`, `#authoring`, with `#caretInStory` for a header). Criterion 1 is ticked on `packages/engine/test/fe-135-136-core-authoring.test.ts` (a page number with its format and a page count are inserted in the header story through `DocumentCore.run`, and the core's selection stays in the body; a page number in body text is refused, and a header the editor may not change is refused with `no-access`, with nothing changed). Fixer round 2 (B group 1, 2026-11-13): `insertField`, `insertToc` and `insertSymbol` are `AuthoringCommandName`s in `packages/engine/src/api.ts` (arguments `field`, a `FieldArgs` with `story: { id, pos }` for a header or footer, `toc`, `symbol`), exported from `packages/engine/src/headless.ts`; `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` runs criterion 1 through `exec` and `queryCommand` too (passed 2026-11-13). Round 4, lane FE-131-141 (2026-11-13): criterion 2 is ticked for its date half, on `packages/engine/test/layout-commands.test.ts` ('inserts a date field in the body with the shared computation as `cached`', through `DocumentCore.exec`, which reads back `cached` against `dateFieldText`) and `packages/engine/test/fields-toc.test.ts` (the zone and the date source). The story's `title` half has no data source (03 §4.4): `insertField` stores a title only when the caller gives one, so the criterion is met by the date alone.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-18, criterion 4):** the coverage: `packages/element/src/font-coverage.ts` (`loadGlyphCoverage`: reads the manifest of the backend's fonts port, fetches the file of each family the document names and of the default family, and reads its `cmap` table with the converter's own reader, `packages/export-latex/src/fonts/coverage.ts`, reached through the new subpath export `@docedit/export-latex/fonts/coverage` so that the converter and KaTeX stay out of the page; a family used later is read when first asked for; a file or manifest that cannot be read leaves the family unknown, and an unknown family is never flagged), started by `session.ts` after the editor is mounted. The check: `packages/engine/src/glyph-check.ts` (`lacksGlyph`, `missingGlyphs`, `familyAtCaret`; the family of a run is the one the style cascade gives it, through `resolveParagraph` and `resolveRun`), `DocumentCore.setGlyphCoverage`, `DocumentCore.missingGlyphs()` (every character of the body its font lacks, once for each paragraph) and `DocumentCore.glyphNotice`, which `insertSymbol` sets when the inserted character has no glyph in the font at the caret. The shell shows it in the status bar (`packages/ui/src/App.svelte`, notice kind `glyph`, `notice.glyph.missing`): "The font {family} has no glyph for {char}. The PDF takes it from a fallback font if one has it; otherwise the export stops." Tests: `packages/engine/test/fe-136-glyph-check.test.ts` (6 tests, Node.js: a Greek letter in a run set in a Latin-only font is flagged with its family, through a mark and through the paragraph's style chain; a character the font has, an unknown family and a core with no coverage are not flagged; each notice is counted; the missing characters of the body are listed once for each paragraph, and each position holds its character), `packages/element/test/fe-136-glyph-coverage.test.ts` (5 tests, Node.js, with KaTeX's Main face, a real TrueType file that has `A` and `Γ` and no `ω`: the coverage of the named and the default family, a file that cannot be read, a family read on first use, no manifest family, the families a document names) and `packages/element/e2e/fe-136-glyph-notice.spec.ts` (the real element: the host's manifest names the file, which a route serves; inserting `Γ` in a run set in that font raises no notice, inserting `ω` raises the notice naming the font and the character in the status bar, and a symbol in a family that has no file in the manifest raises none; `DOCEDIT_DEMO_PORT=58136 pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/element/playwright.config.ts packages/element/e2e/fe-136-glyph-notice.spec.ts --project=chromium --project=firefox --project=webkit --retries=0 --workers=1`: 3 passed, 2026-11-18). `ui/test/notices.test.ts` now gives the two parameters of the new notice.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-18, criteria 3 and 5):** criterion 3, the entries: `packages/engine/src/toc-entries.ts` (`tocEntries`: for `headings`, the paragraphs of the body whose style chain has an `outlineLevel` from `minLevel` to `maxLevel` (`outlineLevelOf` of the model), with the list label of a numbered heading; for a sequence name such as `Figure`, the blocks that hold a `seq` field of that name, with the field shown as its computed number; no page numbers, as the web view shows no pages, 02 §7), `packages/engine/src/view/toc-view.ts` (`tocPlugin`, `tocView`, registered in `packages/engine/src/view/web.ts`: the entries are computed when the document changes and drawn as a non-editable list; nothing is stored). Tests: `packages/engine/test/toc-entries.test.ts` (5 tests, Node.js: level range, level limits, a heading through the style chain, an empty heading left out, a list of one caption sequence with computed numbers and others ignored) and `packages/engine/src/view/fe-136-toc.universal.test.ts` (a table of contents and a list of figures on a live view: a new heading, an edited heading, a new captioned figure with its computed number and a removed heading are followed; `pnpm exec vitest run --project browser <file>`: 3 passed, Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 2026-11-18). Insertion with level limits was ticked before for `insertToc` (`packages/engine/test/fields-toc.test.ts`, `layout-commands.test.ts`). Criterion 5: `insertEquation` in `packages/engine/src/equation-commands.ts`, registered in `packages/engine/src/core.ts` and `packages/engine/src/api.ts` (`CommandName` `insertEquation`, argument `newEquation: { display }`): it inserts an empty inline `equation` at the caret or an empty `equation_block` after the caret's block, and `requestEquationEdit` asks the shell to open the math editor on it. `packages/engine/test/fe-136-insert-equation.test.ts` (5 tests, Node.js: inline and block, the request names the new node, a second request is counted, refused without a change or a request when the person may not edit, argument needed) and `packages/element/e2e/fe-136-insert-equation.spec.ts` (the real element under the strict policy of FE-070: the command opens the math editor on the new empty equation with focus in the source field, and confirming `a^2+b^2` stores it on the block; 1 of 1 passed in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit on 2026-11-18, port 57136).

**Notes:** Lane w1 (2026-11-18, second pass): criterion 4 is ticked (see its Evidence); the statement below that it stays open is superseded. Readings taken, not stated by the spec: the editor's check is against the font of the run at the caret alone, not the fallback chain, so it flags a character the PDF may still draw from a fallback font (the notice says so, and a final export stops only when no font in the chain has the character, 09 §3.2); the symbol command has no shell button or picker yet, so the flag is reached through the core's `insertSymbol` command and the character a person types is not flagged (`missingGlyphs()` lists those and is not shown in any pane); the coverage of the regular face is used for every weight and style of a family; the notice names the character and the host's font name, which the person has just chosen (it names no hidden content); a host with no `fonts` port, or whose CSP forbids `fetch` of the font files, gets no flag. Lane w1 (2026-11-18, first pass): criteria 3 and 5 are ticked (see Evidence); criterion 4 stayed open then (flagging a character the current font lacks needs the font coverage of the document's fonts in the editor, which the engine does not hold; the export converter has `fontCoverage`, the editor has no such data) and the criterion 2 title half stays as before. Readings taken, not stated by the spec: a list of "tables" works for a paragraph that holds a `seq` field named `Table`, as the CDM has no table caption node; the accessible name of a list of a sequence is "List of " plus the lower-cased name and an `s` (so `Figure` gives "List of figures"), which suits English names only; an equation inserted and then cancelled in the math editor stays in the document with an empty source (no step removes it); the equation command is registered as a block-only command, so it is unavailable in suggestion mode with the `suggestion` reason; `tocEntries` walks the document on every change while a `toc` is present, unmeasured. Earlier notes follow. 03 gives the `title` field no data source; recorded in the findings note. Criterion 2 stays open for the title: `insertField` stores the title only when the caller gives one, and otherwise leaves `cached` null. Criterion 3 stays open: the view draws the entries and keeps them up to date, which is not built. Criterion 4 stays open: flagging a character the current font lacks needs font coverage data (09 §3.2), not built. Criterion 5 is FE-070's (the math editor, not built). Decisions taken here, not in the spec, recorded in `docs/adr/0131-engine-2-backlog-batch.md`: the date takes the server instant and the workflow zone as arguments; a `toc` defaults to levels 1 to 3 and `headings`, the CDM's defaults; the format is checked against the CDM pattern and stored as given, not rendered. The commands are in the core's command table (`core.ts`, `api.ts`). Decision of the fixer round, not in the spec: a page field's place in a story is given as the story's identifier and a position counted as the story's sub-editor counts it, because the core's own selection never leaves the body. The shell's `insertField` (`packages/ui/src/core-api.ts`, FRW-072) is a form field, a different command under the same name; the element does not route it to the core's `exec`, and the two must not be joined without renaming one.

### FE-137 Set up pages, headers, footers and the watermark

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | M | must | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (headers and footers, watermark); 03 §4.1 (`page_setup`), §4.2; 07 §5.3 · **Requirements:** R1, R3, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-057, CDM-020, CDM-021, PRM-012

As an author, I want to set the paper size, orientation, margins, header and footer layout and watermark of each page section, so that the PDF comes out as intended.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a page section, when I choose a paper size, orientation and margins, then its `page_setup` stores them in twentieths of a point.
- [x] Given different first page or different odd and even pages, when I turn them on, then the matching header and footer stories are created and opened in the header panel (FE-057).
- [x] Given header and footer distances and the gutter, when I set them, then they are stored.
- [x] Given page number start and format, when I set them, then page fields follow them.
- [x] Given a watermark text, when I set it, then `watermark` is stored and the PDF prints it (EXP-036).
- [x] Given page setups are definitions, when anyone changes one, then the rights of 07 §5.3 apply (PRM-012), so a region-only editor cannot.

**Evidence:** criteria 1 and 3: `packages/engine/src/core.ts` registers `setPageSetup` as a document API command (`run`, `exec`, `queryCommand` with `pageSetup: { id, changes }`; `PageSetupArgs` in `api.ts`); it asks the region guard's `canWrite` for the `defs` scope. `packages/engine/test/authoring-commands.test.ts` goes through `DocumentCore`: A4 landscape with margins is stored in twips (11906 by 16838), header and footer distances and the gutter are stored, an editor without `edit` on `defs` gets `no-access` and nothing is changed or queued, and an out-of-range value or an unknown page setup changes nothing. The value rules: `packages/engine/src/page-setup-commands.ts` and `packages/engine/test/page-setup-commands.test.ts` (a property over the margin range). Reviewed and unticked on 2026-11-13 because nothing reached the core; re-ticked after the command was registered. Criterion 6 (engine round 8): `packages/engine/test/authoring-commands.test.ts` ('is unavailable to an editor without edit on the definitions (07 §5.3), and changes nothing') and `packages/engine/test/page-setup-commands.test.ts` ('a region-only editor cannot change a page setup definition (07 §5.3, PRM-012)'), which checks the guard at the definition's own position. Criterion 5, the storing half (lane FE-131-141, round 4, 2026-11-13): `packages/engine/test/fe-137-watermark.test.ts` ('stores the watermark text on the page setup, and clears it with null'; 'refuses a watermark outside 1 to 255 characters and changes nothing'; 'is unavailable to an editor without edit on the definitions, and stores nothing'). Criterion 5 stays unticked: the PDF that prints the watermark is EXP-036's.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-18), criteria 2, 4 and 5:** criterion 2: the core's `run('setPageSetup')` now opens the header of a variant that the call turned on in the panel, after the stories are made in the same transaction (`packages/engine/src/core.ts`, `run` and `#headerVariantsInUse`; it calls `editStory('header', variant)` of FE-057). `packages/engine/test/fe-137-variant-wiring.test.ts` (7 tests, Node.js: 'opens the new variant header in the header panel', the even variant alone, and no panel when nothing is turned on or the flag was already on) with `fe-057-variants.test.ts`; the panel's own display is FE-057's browser test (`packages/element/e2e/fe-057-panel.spec.ts`). Criterion 4: the start and format are stored (`packages/engine/test/page-setup-commands.test.ts`) and the page field follows them in the layout: `packages/layout/src/fe-137-page-number-fields.test.ts` (3 tests, Node.js, `pnpm exec vitest run --project node packages/layout/src/fe-137-page-number-fields.test.ts`, passed 2026-11-18): a CDM page setup read through `toFlow` with a header and footer story holding `page` and `pageCount` fields gives 'Page 1, 2, 3' by default, 5, 6, 7 from a start of 5 (page count stays the number of pages), and iii, iv, v, vi in `lowerRoman` from 3; the painting of the field text is FE-087's (`fe-087-fields.universal.test.ts`). Criterion 5: stored as above; the PDF prints it: EXP-036 criterion 2 is ticked, and `packages/export-latex/src/exp-036-watermark.compile.test.ts` and `exp-036-watermark.tagging.compile.test.ts` passed in the Podman sandbox on 2026-11-18 (3 tests, 43 s).

**Notes (lane w1, 2026-11-18):** every criterion is ticked. Limit: the page-field test lays out a CDM envelope built for the test, not one edited through the core and passed on to the layout in one run, because the engine package does not depend on the layout package. The opening of the panel after turning on a variant picks the first variant turned on (first page before odd and even pages) and does not open the footer; the footer is one button away in the panel.

**Notes (lane FE-131-141, round 5, 2026-11-13):** criterion 2, the engine part only. `packages/engine/src/variant-turn-on.ts` (`turnOnVariants`) builds one transaction that sets `differentFirstPage` or `differentOddEven` and creates the header and footer stories of that variant, with the variant attribute naming each; a story that exists is kept, a `writable` of false or an identifier already in use returns null. `packages/engine/test/fe-137-variant-turn-on.test.ts` (5 tests, passed 2026-11-13). Criterion 2 stays unticked for two reasons. No command calls the module: the core's `setPageSetup` still sets the flag alone, so no user path creates the stories, and wiring it needs an edit to the shared `core.ts` and `api.ts`, which this lane did not make. The opening of the header panel is the view's (FE-057), as the footnote panel was. The story identifiers are supplied by the caller, not chosen by the module. Criteria 4 (page number start and format) and 5 are not touched in this round. Lane U-FE-129-141 (2026-11-13): the wiring is made. `setPageSetup` takes an optional `storyIdOf` and, when a flag is turned on, builds the stories with `turnOnVariants` in the same transaction; the core passes its identifier source (`packages/engine/src/page-setup-commands.ts`, `packages/engine/src/core.ts`). `packages/engine/test/fe-137-variant-wiring.test.ts` (4 tests, passed with `pnpm exec vitest run`). Criterion 2 stays unticked: the opening of the header panel is the view's (FE-057).

**Notes (lane V-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13):** criterion 4 is partly made in the model. `pageNumberOf` and `pageNumberText` (`packages/model/src/numbering/page-numbers.ts`) give a section's page number and print it in its `pageNumberFormat`, through the shared `formatNumber`; `fieldText` computes a `page` field, and a `pageCount` field in decimal, once the typesetter gives the number (`packages/model/src/structure/field-text.ts`). Tests: `packages/model/src/numbering/v-fe-129-141-page-numbers.test.ts` (7 tests passed in Node.js). Criterion 4 stays unticked: nothing in the layout or the painter gives the page its number and section yet, so no page field in the web view or the PDF follows the start and format. The `pageCount` format is a proposed reading, recorded in `docs/adr/0308-lane-V-FE-129-141.md`. Criterion 2 needs the header panel (FE-057).

**Engine-lane notes (round 1, not decided here):** paper sizes are the two presets A4 and US Letter; the spec names no others here, so further sizes are an open point. Orientation is stored as given and does not swap width and height. Criterion 2 (header and footer stories, the first-page and odd-page variants) and criterion 4 (page fields follow the start and format) are not built. Criterion 5 (the PDF prints the watermark) belongs to EXP-036. Criterion 6 stays open: the core's command refuses an editor without `edit` on `defs` through the region guard, but PRM-012 enforcement is the authority's and is not tested here. The page setup dialog is UX's and is not built.

### FE-138 Zoom the editing surface

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 1b | S | should | Author |

**Spec:** 13 §2 (zoom), §3 (status bar); 02 §10 · **Requirements:** R1, R11, R14 · **Depends on:** FE-066, FE-106

As an author, I want to zoom the editing surface in and out, so that I can read small text or see more of the document.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given the web view, when I change the zoom level from the status bar, a shortcut or the view commands, then the content scales and reflows to the available width while the interface does not.
- [x] Given browser zoom as well, then the web view still reflows (FE-106) and the product's zoom works on top of it.
- [x] Given the read view or the PDF panel, when I zoom, then the pages scale and stay sharp.
- [x] Given the keyboard, then zoom in, zoom out and reset are reachable without a pointer.

**Evidence (lane w1, 2026-11-18, criterion 3):** `packages/element/e2e/fe-138-pdf-zoom.spec.ts` (the PDF panel in the sandboxed viewer frame, on a screen with a device pixel ratio of 2; `DOCEDIT_DEMO_PORT=58138 pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/element/playwright.config.ts packages/element/e2e/fe-138-pdf-zoom.spec.ts --project=chromium --project=firefox --project=webkit --retries=0 --workers=1`: 3 passed, 2026-11-18): at 100 per cent the page canvas has one backing pixel for each device pixel it covers (its width and height are its CSS size times the ratio); three steps of Zoom in (125, 150, 200 per cent) double the CSS size and the backing store with it, so the page is drawn again, not stretched; Zoom out to 50 per cent halves both, and the backing store still equals the CSS size times the ratio. The viewer draws each page again at the zoom with PDF.js and the ratio (`packages/element/src/pdf/viewer/main.ts`, `draw`). The read view (`view="read"`) opens the same viewer frame (`packages/element/src/read-view-element.ts`, `PdfViewerFrame`), so its pages have the same zoom controls and the same drawing; its own run is `packages/element/e2e/fe-072-read-view.spec.ts`, which does not zoom.

**Evidence (lane V-FE-129-141, criterion 2):** `packages/ui/e2e/v-fe-129-141-browser-zoom.spec.ts` ('reflows at 320 CSS pixels, the WCAG reflow width'; 'reflows at 640 CSS pixels with the product zoom at 150 per cent on top'; 'keeps the product zoom working at the 320 CSS pixel width'). Run with `DOCEDIT_UI_PORT=57080 DOCEDIT_BROWSERS=<browser> pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/ui/playwright.config.ts e2e/v-fe-129-141-browser-zoom.spec.ts --retries=0`: 3 of 3 passed in Chromium, and 6 of 6 across Firefox and WebKit. Browser zoom is simulated by the CSS viewport width: a 640 CSS pixel window is what 200 per cent shows on a 1280 pixel window, and 320 CSS pixels is the WCAG 1.4.10 width. The browser's own zoom control was not driven, which is a limit of the harness.

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13):** `packages/ui/e2e/fe-138-zoom.spec.ts`, run with `pnpm exec playwright test -c packages/ui/playwright.config.ts packages/ui/e2e/fe-138-zoom.spec.ts` (Chromium, Firefox, WebKit: 6 passed). Criterion 1: the status bar keeps its size and font size at 150 per cent, the editing surface grows and the body host has no horizontal overflow; a 360 px window at 200 per cent has no horizontal overflow; no core command runs. Criterion 4: Ctrl+F6 reaches the status bar, then Tab and Enter reach and use Zoom out, Zoom in and Reset zoom (`packages/ui/src/components/StatusBar.svelte`). No keyboard shortcut is bound to the zoom commands (`packages/ui/src/commands/builtin.ts` has no `keys` for `view.zoom*`); the choice of keys is still open. Criteria 2 and 3 are not ticked: browser zoom was not driven, and the read view and PDF panel are not built.

**Notes (lane w1, 2026-11-18):** criterion 3 is ticked on the viewer's own zoom (its buttons and the keys `+`, `=` and `-`, FE-071 criterion 5): the PDF panel and the read view show the typeset PDF in the viewer frame, whose zoom is the frame's, not the status bar's. The status bar's zoom (`view.zoomIn` and the others) scales the web view's editing surface alone; it does not reach into the viewer frame, which is another origin and takes only the PDF and its strings (`packages/element/src/pdf/messages.ts`), so in the read view the status bar's buttons do not scale the pages and the person uses the viewer's buttons. Whether the two should be one control is not decided here. "Sharp" is measured as one backing pixel for each device pixel of the page canvas at a device pixel ratio of 2, in three engines; no person looked at the pages, and no ratio other than 2 was run. Earlier notes follow.

**Notes (lane engine-2, round 4, 2026-11-13):** no zoom state or command exists in `packages/engine`, and the spec puts zoom in the view (02 §10). All four criteria need the web view, the status bar or the keyboard, so none is met by a headless engine test. Nothing ticked; no zoom model added to the engine, because the level and its steps are not stated in the spec. Lane U-FE-129-141 (2026-11-13): the zoom is the shell's (`packages/ui/src/shell/zoom.ts`, applied as `style:zoom` on `.de-body-host` in `packages/ui/src/App.svelte`). Criterion 4 is not met: `view.zoomIn`, `view.zoomOut` and `view.zoomReset` in `packages/ui/src/commands/builtin.ts` have no `keys`, so no shortcut reaches them. Choosing the keys is a UX and root decision, not made here. Criteria 1 to 3 need a browser run of the demo shell, not run in this lane. Lane U-FE-129-141 (2026-11-13): criteria 1 and 4 are ticked on the browser run in Evidence; criterion 4 is met by Tab and Enter on the status bar's buttons, which the criterion's "without a pointer" allows, and the shortcut keys stay an open UX decision (the spec names none). Criterion 2 needs browser zoom, which the Playwright harness does not set, and criterion 3 needs the read view or PDF panel (FE-071, FE-072), not built.

### FE-139 Show preserved content with its fallback and change it only as a whole

| Type | Phase | Size | Priority | Persona |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| story | 2 | M | must | Author |

**Spec:** 03 §4.6; ADR 0017 (part 3); 02 §5.2, §10 · **Requirements:** R1, R5, R11 · **Depends on:** CDM-051, EXP-117

As an author, I want the charts, text boxes and other content of an imported Word file that the editor cannot edit shown in place, so that I can see and keep them while I edit everything else.

**Acceptance criteria**

- [x] Given a `preserved_block` or `preserved_inline` atom, when it is shown in the web view, then its fallback image or text is displayed at its display size with a visible label naming its kind, for example "Chart, from Word, not editable here".
- [ ] Given such an atom, when a person with `edit` in its scope selects, moves, copies, cuts or deletes it, then it behaves as one unit; no command, input method or paste edits inside it.
- [ ] Given a screen reader, when the caret reaches the atom, then its label and fallback text are announced.
- [ ] Given find and replace, when it runs, then it does not match inside preserved content and reports how many atoms it skipped.
- [x] Given the fragment's XML, when the atom is shown, then the editor never parses or renders it; only the fallback asset or text reaches the page.
- [ ] Given an atom without a fallback image, when the accessibility checker runs, then it reports the atom as content without a text alternative.

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, 2026-11-13, criteria 1, 3 and 5):** `packages/engine/src/fe-139-preserved-render.universal.test.ts` ('shows a label naming the kind and the source, then the fallback text, in a block atom'; 'gives the atom an accessible name that holds its label and its fallback text'; 'is not editable: the atom and the content inside it carry contenteditable false'; 'never renders the XML of the fragment, only the label and the fallback text'; 'shows the label alone when there is no fallback text'), run with `pnpm vitest run --project browser packages/engine/src/fe-139-preserved-render.universal.test.ts` (Chromium, Firefox and WebKit: 15 passed). The rendering is `preservedDOM` in `packages/engine/src/schema.ts`, which sets no `style` attribute (the strict policy, 02 §3). Criterion 5 is ticked on that test. The Node tests `packages/engine/test/fe-139-preserved-find.test.ts`, `fe-139-find-count.test.ts`, `web-view-safety.test.ts` and `packages/engine/src/sec-006-link-render.universal.test.ts` still pass (27 passed in Node.js, 3 skipped). Criterion 1 stays open: the display size needs a CSS rule from the element (no inline style is allowed), and the fallback image is not drawn, only the text. Criterion 3 stays open: the accessible name is tested, but the announcement when the caret reaches the atom is not run in a screen reader. Criterion 2 stays open: the unit behaviour under select, move, copy and delete is not tested in a browser.

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, round 7, 2026-11-14, criterion 2 in part, not ticked):** `packages/engine/src/fe-139-preserved-unit.universal.test.ts` (3 tests on a live `EditorView` over the editing schema: the atom holds no content and its DOM is not editable; a whole-atom selection is deleted in one step and the text around it stays; a text selection that runs from before the atom to after it takes the atom whole). Run with `pnpm vitest run --project browser packages/engine/src/fe-139-preserved-unit.universal.test.ts`: Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 9 passed. Criterion 2 also names move, copy and cut: a cut is a delete here, and a move is a drag, which is the view's; copy of the atom is not tested, so the criterion stays unticked.

**Evidence (lane U-FE-129-141, round 8, 2026-11-14, criterion 2, copy and cut, not ticked):** `packages/engine/test/fe-139-atom-copy.test.ts` (2 tests, Node.js, `pnpm vitest run packages/engine/test/fe-139-atom-copy.test.ts`, passed): a whole-atom selection copies with the atom and its fallback text in the payload and keeps the atom in the document; a whole-atom cut removes the atom in one step and its payload keeps the atom. Criterion 2 stays unticked: move is a drag, which is the view's.

**Evidence:** criterion 4, the search half (lane FE-131-141, round 4, 2026-11-13): `packages/engine/test/fe-139-preserved-find.test.ts` ('matches the text paragraph but not the fallback text of a preserved atom'; 'reports how many preserved atoms the query would have matched and the search skipped'; 'skips nothing when no preserved fallback holds the query, and nothing for an empty query'; 'counts each preserved atom once, however many times its fallback holds the query'; 'a document without preserved content skips nothing'), over `packages/engine/src/preserved-find.ts` (`skippedPreservedAtoms`) and `findMatches` in `packages/engine/src/search.ts`. Criterion 4 stays unticked: the find panel that shows the count is the view's. Lane U-FE-129-141 (2026-11-13): `DocumentCore.skippedPreservedFind()` (`packages/engine/src/core.ts`) returns `skippedPreservedAtoms` for the current query, so the core's find state has the count; `packages/engine/test/fe-139-find-count.test.ts` (3 tests, passed with `pnpm exec vitest run`, with `search.test.ts` and `search-highlight.test.ts` still passing). The count is not added to the published `FindState`, which would change its shape for existing consumers.
